class="col-md-3"><img src="https://cdn.webshopapp.com/shops/312703/files/353624218/image.jpg" alt="" class="img-responsive"></div><div class="col-md-9"><div class="content"><p>IPA staat voor India Pale Ale. Aan deze, van oorsprong, Engelse biersoort wordt meer hop toegevoegd dan bij normaal bier. Dat zorgt voor meer bitterheid. Ook proef je veel fruitige smaken, die de bittere smaken weer nuanceren. Dit maakt IPA bier een populaire stijl binnen de speciaalbieren.<br /><br />Bij Bierloods22 kun je terecht voor IPA speciaalbieren van brouwers van over de hele wereld. Ook hebben we <a href="https://www.bierloods22.nl/nl/bierpakket/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">pakketten met IPA bieren</a>. Er zijn veel verschillende soorten IPA speciaalbier zoals de klassieke IPA, NEIPA, DIPA, TIPA &amp; Black IPA. Bij Bierloods22 kun je terecht voor IPA's in al deze en andere stijlen. Hieronder zie je ons aanbod.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="row"><div class="col-md-12 margin-top"><div class="custom-filters-holder"><div class=""><form id="sort_filters" en bestelnummer in.</p><form id="withdrawal-lookup-form" novalidate><div class="withdrawal-form-field"><label for="withdrawal-email">E-mail</label><input type="email" id="withdrawal-email" name="withdrawal_lookup[email]" placeholder=email@voorbeeld.nl autocomplete="email" /><span class="withdrawal-field-error" id="withdrawal-email-error"></span></div><div class="withdrawal-form-field"><label for="withdrawal-order-number">Ordernummer</label><input type="text" id="withdrawal-order-number" name="withdrawal_lookup[order_number]" placeholder="bijvoorbeeld 123456" autocomplete="off" /><span class="withdrawal-field-error" id="withdrawal-order-error"></span></div></form></div><div class="withdrawal-modal-footer"><button class="withdrawal-btn-cancel" type="button">Annuleren</button><button class="withdrawal-btn-submit" type="submit" form="withdrawal-lookup-form" id="withdrawal-submit-btn">Bestelling zoeken</button></div></div><div class="withdrawal-success" id="withdrawal-modal-success"><div class="withdrawal-modal-header"><p class="withdrawal-modal-title" id="withdrawal-modal-title-success">Controleer uw e-mail</p></div><div class="withdrawal-modal-body"><p class="withdrawal-modal-subtitle">Als uw gegevens overeenkomen met een bestelling, ontvangt u een e-mail met een link om deze te bekijken en te beheren.</p></div><div class="withdrawal-modal-footer"><button class="withdrawal-btn-submit withdrawal-btn-got-it" type="button">Ik snap het</button></div></div></div></div><script>
(function () {
  var wrapper   = document.getElementById('withdrawal-lookup-modal-wrapper');
  var submitBtn = document.getElementById('withdrawal-submit-btn');
  var emailInput     = document.getElementById('withdrawal-email');
  var orderInput     = document.getElementById('withdrawal-order-number');
  var emailError     = document.getElementById('withdrawal-email-error');
  var orderError     = document.getElementById('withdrawal-order-error');
  var formSection    = document.getElementById('withdrawal-modal-form-section');
  var successSection = document.getElementById('withdrawal-modal-success');

  function openModal() {
    wrapper.classList.add('is-open');
    emailInput.focus();
  }

  function closeModal() {
    wrapper.classList.remove('is-open');
    resetModal();
  }

  function resetModal() {
    emailInput.value = '';
    orderInput.value = '';
    clearError(emailInput, emailError);
    clearError(orderInput, orderError);
    formSection.style.display = '';
    successSection.style.display = 'none';
    submitBtn.disabled = false;
    submitBtn.textContent = 'Bestelling zoeken';
    wrapper.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'withdrawal-modal-title-form');
  }

  function showError(input, errorEl, msg) {
    input.classList.add('has-error');
    errorEl.textContent = msg;
    errorEl.style.display = 'block';
  }

  function clearError(input, errorEl) {
    input.classList.remove('has-error');
    errorEl.textContent = '';
    errorEl.style.display = 'none';
  }

  function isValidEmail(val) {
    return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(val);
  }

  function validate() {
    var valid = true;
    var email = emailInput.value.trim();
    var order = orderInput.value.trim();

    clearError(emailInput, emailError);
    clearError(orderInput, orderError);

    if (!email) {
      showError(emailInput, emailError, 'E-mailadres is vereist');
      valid = false;
    } else if (!isValidEmail(email)) {
      showError(emailInput, emailError, 'Geef een geldig e-mail adres op alstublieft.');
      valid = false;
    }

    if (!order) {
      showError(orderInput, orderError, 'Bestelnummer is verplicht.');
      valid = false;
    }

    return valid;
  }

  function showSuccess() {
    formSection.style.display = 'none';
    successSection.style.display = 'block';
    wrapper.setAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'withdrawal-modal-title-success');
  }

  function submit() {
    if (!validate()) return;

    submitBtn.disabled = true;
    submitBtn.textContent = 'Searching...';

    var formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('withdrawal_lookup[email]', emailInput.value.trim());
    formData.append('withdrawal_lookup[order_number]', orderInput.value.trim());

    fetch("https://www.bierloods22.nl/nl/services/withdrawalLookup/", {
      method: 'POST',
      body: formData,
    })
    .then(showSuccess)
    .catch(showSuccess);
  }

  // Open modal when any link to withdrawal-lookup is clicked
  document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var el = e.target.closest('a');
    if (el && el.href && (el.href.indexOf('withdrawal-lookup') !== -1)) {
      e.preventDefault();
      openModal();
    }
  });

  // Close on backdrop, close button, cancel button, got-it button
  wrapper.querySelector('.withdrawal-modal-background').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  wrapper.querySelectorAll('.withdrawal-modal-close, .withdrawal-btn-cancel, .withdrawal-btn-got-it').forEach(function (el) {
    el.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  });

  // ESC key
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && wrapper.classList.contains('is-open')) closeModal();
  });

  // Submit
  document.getElementById('withdrawal-lookup-form').addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    submit();
  });

  // Live clear errors on input
  emailInput.addEventListener('input', function () { clearError(emailInput, emailError); });
  orderInput.addEventListener('input', function () { clearError(orderInput, orderError); });
}());
</script><!-- [END] 'blocks/body.rain' --><div class="newsletter gray-bg"><h3><i class="fa fa-paper-plane" aria-hidden="true"></i> Meld je aan voor onze nieuwsbrief
